#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

The update is a pair of radius-1 stencils. I’m using a fused CUDA kernel that builds the needed chemical-potential halo in shared memory, so each time step needs one launch and one global write instead of materializing and rereading the full intermediate field. Boundary clamping and the original initialization/output behavior remain unchanged.

+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;

+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \

+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess)                                    \
+            cudaFailure(#call, cuda_check_error);                               \

+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {
+    return max(0, min(value, extent - 1));
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+namespace {
+
+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \

+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+namespace {
+
+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+
+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+namespace {
+
+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
+void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, size_t nx, size_t ny,

     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
             for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;

+                    static_cast<double>(volume);
+                c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
             }

     }
 }

+bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {

             return false;

-    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {

+    char* end = nullptr;

+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

codex
The first CUDA build is correct on both an irregular grid and the default case; sampled values agree with the CPU reference to floating-point roundoff, and the default run improves from about 154 MCellUpdates/s to 3,497 MCellUpdates/s on the available RTX 3090. I’m checking resource usage and edge-size behavior now, since shared-memory occupancy and partial tiles are the main remaining performance/correctness risks.

+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

-    }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+namespace {
+
+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

-    
-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);

-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);

-    return cxx + cyy + czz;
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (call);                            \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess)                                    \
+            cudaFailure(#call, cuda_check_error);                               \
+    } while (false)

+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {
+    return max(0, min(value, extent - 1));
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

+namespace {
+
+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* operation, cudaError_t error) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+namespace {

+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
+constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
+constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
+constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
+constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;
+

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
