-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

codex
A direct comparison showed the fused shared-memory version was compute/shared-bandwidth limited: about 4.6 billion updates/s at \(256^3\). The simpler two-kernel CUDA stencil reaches about 8.1 billion updates/s because the GPU cache already captures neighbor reuse efficiently. I’m switching to that faster design; it still keeps all iteration data resident on the GPU.

    17	// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
    18	// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
    19	// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
    20	constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
    21	constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
    22	constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
    23	constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;
    24	constexpr int C_Y = BLOCK_Y + 4;
    25	constexpr int C_Z = BLOCK_Z + 4;
    26	constexpr int MU_X = BLOCK_X + 2;
    27	constexpr int MU_Y = BLOCK_Y + 2;
    28	constexpr int MU_Z = BLOCK_Z + 2;
    29	constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;
    30	constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
    31	constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;

    46	__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {
    47	    return max(0, min(value, extent - 1));

    50	__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
    51	    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;

    53	
    54	__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
    55	    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;

    57	
    58	// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
    59	// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
    60	// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
    61	__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
    62	void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
    63	                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
    64	                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
    65	                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
    66	                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
    67	    extern __shared__ double shared[];
    68	    double* const cTile = shared;
    69	    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
    70	
    71	    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
    72	                     threadIdx.x;
    73	    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
    74	    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
    75	    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
    76	
    77	    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
    78	    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
    79	    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
    80	        const int lx = i % C_X;
    81	        const int q = i / C_X;
    82	        const int ly = q % C_Y;
    83	        const int lz = q / C_Y;
    84	        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
    85	        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
    86	        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
    87	        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
    88	                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
    89	    }
    90	    __syncthreads();
    91	
    92	    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
    93	    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
    94	    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
    95	    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
    96	        const int mx = i % MU_X;
    97	        const int q = i / MU_X;
    98	        const int my = q % MU_Y;
    99	        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
   100	
   101	        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
   102	        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
   103	        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
   104	        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
   105	        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
   106	        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
   107	
   108	        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
   109	        const double cxx =
   110	            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
   111	             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
   112	             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
   113	        const double cyy =
   114	            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
   115	             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
   116	             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
   117	        const double czz =
   118	            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
   119	             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
   120	             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
   121	
   122	        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
   123	                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
   124	                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
   125	    }
   126	    __syncthreads();
   127	
   128	    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
   129	    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
   130	    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
   131	    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
   132	        return;
   133	
   134	    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
   135	    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
   136	    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
   137	    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
   138	    const double mux =
   139	        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
   140	         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
   141	    const double muy =
   142	        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
   143	         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
   144	    const double muz =
   145	        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
   146	         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
   147	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
   148	                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
   149	    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
   150	                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
   151	                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
   152	}
   153	
   154	void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, size_t nx, size_t ny,
   155	                             size_t nz) {
   156	    const size_t volume = nx * ny * nz;
   157	    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
   158	        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
   159	            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
   160	                const size_t index = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
   161	                const double pseudo =
   162	                    (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
   163	                    static_cast<double>(volume);
   164	                c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   165	            }
   166	        }
   167	    }
   168	}
   169	
   170	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {
   171	    for (double value : c) {
   172	        if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
   173	            std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   174	            return false;
   175	        }
   176	    }
   177	    const auto limits = std::minmax_element(c.begin(), c.end());
   178	    std::printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", *limits.first,
   179	                *limits.second);
   180	    if (*limits.second > 10.0 || *limits.first < -10.0) {
   181	        std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   182	        return false;
   183	    }
   184	    return true;
   185	}
   186	
   187	void printUsage(const char* program) {
   188	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
   189	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   190	    std::printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   191	    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   192	    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   193	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   194	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   195	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   196	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   197	}
   198	
   199	bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
   200	    errno = 0;
   201	    char* end = nullptr;
   202	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   203	    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
   204	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
   205	        return false;
   270	    std::printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   271	    std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   272	
   273	    constexpr double dx = 1.0;
   274	    constexpr double dy = 1.0;
   275	    constexpr double dz = 1.0;
   276	    constexpr double dt = 0.01;
   277	    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   278	    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   279	    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
   280	    constexpr double gamma = 0.5;
   281	    constexpr double diffusion = 1.0;
   282	
   283	    std::vector<double> concentration(gridSize);
   284	    std::printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   285	    initializeConcentration(concentration, nx, ny, nz);
   286	
   287	    // Device allocation and the initial upload are setup costs, as allocation
   288	    // and initialization were outside the timed region in the CPU benchmark.
   289	    double* deviceCold = nullptr;
   290	    double* deviceNew = nullptr;
   291	    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(double);
   292	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceCold, bytes));
   293	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceNew, bytes));
   294	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceCold, concentration.data(), bytes,
   295	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   296	
   297	    const dim3 block(BLOCK_X, BLOCK_Y, BLOCK_Z);
   298	    const dim3 grid((nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X,
   299	                    (ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,
   300	                    (nz + BLOCK_Z - 1) / BLOCK_Z);
   301	    const size_t sharedBytes = (C_TILE_SIZE + MU_TILE_SIZE) * sizeof(double);
   302	
   303	    std::printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   304	    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
   305	    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
   306	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
   307	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
   308	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
   309	    for (int step = 0; step < iterations; ++step) {
   310	        cahnHilliardStep<<<grid, block, sharedBytes>>>(
   311	            deviceCold, deviceNew, static_cast<int>(nx), static_cast<int>(ny),
   312	            static_cast<int>(nz), nx * ny, dt * diffusion, 1.0 / (dx * dx),
   313	            1.0 / (dy * dy), 1.0 / (dz * dz), gamma, eAA, eBB, eAB);
   314	        std::swap(deviceCold, deviceNew);
   315	    }
   316	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   317	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
   318	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
   319	    float elapsedMs = 0.0f;
   320	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop));
   321	
   322	    std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedMs);
   323	    const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations;
   324	    const double mcups = cellUpdates / (static_cast<double>(elapsedMs) * 1000.0);
   325	    std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   326	
   327	    if (printResults || validate) {
   328	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(concentration.data(), deviceCold, bytes,
   329	                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   330	    }
   331	    if (printResults)
   332	        print_results(concentration, "Concentration");
   333	
   334	    bool valid = true;
   335	    if (validate) {
   336	        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   337	        valid = validateResult(concentration);
   338	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
   342	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
   343	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceCold));
   344	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceNew));
   345	    return valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   346	}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+// A 16x8x4 output tile gives coalesced x accesses and 512-way parallelism.
+// The two-cell c halo is sufficient to form the one-cell chemical-potential
+// halo needed by the Cahn-Hilliard update.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 16;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 4;
+constexpr int C_X = BLOCK_X + 4;

+constexpr int C_TILE_SIZE = C_X * C_Y * C_Z;
+constexpr int MU_TILE_SIZE = MU_X * MU_Y * MU_Z;

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * C_Y + y) * C_X + x;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ int muTileIndex(int x, int y, int z) {
+    return (z * MU_Y + y) * MU_X + x;
+}
+
+// Each launch performs a complete time step.  Compared with two separate
+// stencil kernels, this avoids storing the full mu field and cuts global-memory
+// traffic by constructing both stencil halos cooperatively in shared memory.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS, 2)
+void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                      double* __restrict__ cnew, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                      size_t plane, double dtD, double invDx2,
+                      double invDy2, double invDz2, double gamma,
+                      double eAA, double eBB, double eAB) {
+    extern __shared__ double shared[];
+    double* const cTile = shared;
+    double* const muTile = shared + C_TILE_SIZE;
+
+    const int lane = (threadIdx.z * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y) * BLOCK_X +
+                     threadIdx.x;
+    const int originX = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X;
+    const int originY = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y;
+    const int originZ = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z;
+
+    // Load c with a radius-two clamped halo.  Linear cooperative loading keeps
+    // all threads useful even for boundary and partial blocks.
+    for (int i = lane; i < C_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int lx = i % C_X;
+        const int q = i / C_X;
+        const int ly = q % C_Y;
+        const int lz = q / C_Y;
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + lx - 2, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + ly - 2, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + lz - 2, nz);
+        cTile[i] = cold[static_cast<size_t>(gz) * plane +
+                        static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Compute mu for the output tile and its radius-one halo.  At a domain
+    // edge, center and neighbor coordinates are clamped independently, exactly
+    // matching the original finite-difference boundary condition.
+    for (int i = lane; i < MU_TILE_SIZE; i += THREADS) {
+        const int mx = i % MU_X;
+        const int q = i / MU_X;
+        const int my = q % MU_Y;
+        const int mz = q / MU_Y;
+
+        const int gx = clampIndex(originX + mx - 1, nx);
+        const int gy = clampIndex(originY + my - 1, ny);
+        const int gz = clampIndex(originZ + mz - 1, nz);
+        const int cx = gx - originX + 2;
+        const int cy = gy - originY + 2;
+        const int cz = gz - originZ + 2;
+
+        const double cv = cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, cz)];
+        const double cxx =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx + 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(clampIndex(gx - 1, nx) - originX + 2, cy, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+        const double cyy =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy + 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, clampIndex(gy - 1, ny) - originY + 2, cz)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+        const double czz =
+            (cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz + 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] +
+             cTile[cTileIndex(cx, cy, clampIndex(gz - 1, nz) - originZ + 2)] -
+             2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+
+        muTile[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                           2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);
     }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int x = originX + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = originY + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = originZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const int mx = threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const int my = threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const int mz = threadIdx.z + 1;
+    const double centerMu = muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz)];
+    const double mux =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx + 1, my, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx - 1, my, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDx2;
+    const double muy =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my + 1, mz)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my - 1, mz)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDy2;
+    const double muz =
+        (muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz + 1)] +
+         muTile[muTileIndex(mx, my, mz - 1)] - 2.0 * centerMu) * invDz2;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    cnew[index] = cTile[cTileIndex(threadIdx.x + 2, threadIdx.y + 2,
+                                  threadIdx.z + 2)] +
+                  dtD * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {

+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
-    }
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+// One full warp spans X, making all interior stencil loads coalesced.  The
+// remaining dimensions provide enough blocks and warps to saturate large GPUs.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 4;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;

+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parsePositive(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+namespace {
+
+// One full warp spans X, making all interior stencil loads coalesced.  The
+// remaining dimensions provide enough blocks and warps to saturate large GPUs.
+constexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 4;
+constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 2;
+constexpr int THREADS = BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y * BLOCK_Z;

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (call);                            \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess)                                    \
+            cudaFailure(#call, cuda_check_error);                               \
+    } while (false)
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {
+    return max(0, min(value, extent - 1));
+}
+
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
+}
+
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);

+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;

+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;

+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>

+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }
