+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

codex
The final kernels use 26–28 registers with no spills, retain full occupancy, and pass validation on default, degenerate, irregular, and zero-iteration cases. The default \(64^3 \times 20\) run now reaches about 6.6 billion updates/s on the RTX 3090, versus roughly 154 million for the original CPU code; larger grids sustain about 8 billion updates/s.

    17	// remaining dimensions provide enough blocks and warps to saturate large GPUs.
    18	constexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;
    19	constexpr int BLOCK_Y = 4;
    20	constexpr int BLOCK_Z = 2;

    36	__device__ __forceinline__ int clampIndex(int value, int extent) {
    37	    return max(0, min(value, extent - 1));
    38	}
    39	
    40	__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
    41	void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
    42	                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
    43	                              size_t plane) {
    44	    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
    45	    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
    46	    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
    47	    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
    48	        return;
    49	
    50	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    51	                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
    52	    const double cv = c[index];
    53	    const double cxx =
    54	        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
    55	          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
    56	        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
    57	          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
    58	    const double cyy =
    59	        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    60	          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
    61	        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    62	          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
    63	    const double czz =
    64	        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
    65	          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
    66	        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
    67	          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
    68	    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    69	    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    70	    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    71	    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
    72	                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
    73	                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
    74	}
    75	
    76	__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
    77	void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
    78	                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
    79	                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
    80	                         size_t plane) {
    81	    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
    82	    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
    83	    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
    84	    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
    85	        return;
    86	
    87	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    88	                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
    89	    const double center = mu[index];
    90	    const double mux =
    91	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
    92	           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
    93	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
    94	           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
    95	    const double muy =
    96	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    97	           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
    98	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
    99	           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
   100	    const double muz =
   101	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
   102	           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
   103	        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
   104	           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
   105	    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
   106	}
   107	
   108	void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, size_t nx, size_t ny,
   109	                             size_t nz) {
   110	    const size_t volume = nx * ny * nz;
   111	    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
   112	        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
   113	            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
   114	                const size_t index = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
   115	                const double pseudo =
   116	                    (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
   117	                    static_cast<double>(volume);
   118	                c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   119	            }
   120	        }
   121	    }
   122	}
   123	
   124	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {
   125	    for (double value : c) {
   126	        if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
   127	            std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   128	            return false;
   129	        }
   130	    }
   131	    const auto limits = std::minmax_element(c.begin(), c.end());
   132	    std::printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", *limits.first,
   133	                *limits.second);
   134	    if (*limits.second > 10.0 || *limits.first < -10.0) {
   135	        std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   136	        return false;
   137	    }
   138	    return true;
   139	}

   145	    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   146	    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");

   149	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   150	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   151	}
   152	
   153	bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
   154	    errno = 0;
   155	    char* end = nullptr;
   156	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   157	    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
   158	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
   159	        return false;
   160	    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   161	    return true;
   162	}

   254	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
   255	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
   256	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));

   276

   278	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(concentration.data(), deviceCold, bytes,

   296	    return valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   297	}

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+int main(int ac,char**av){int n=ac>1?atoi(av[1]):256,it=ac>2?atoi(av[2]):100;size_t N=(size_t)n*n*n,b=N*8;double *a,*d,*m;cudaMalloc(&a,b);cudaMalloc(&d,b);cudaMalloc(&m,b);cudaMemset(a,0,b);dim3 B(32,4,2),G((n+31)/32,(n+3)/4,(n+1)/2);cudaEvent_t s,e;cudaEventCreate(&s);cudaEventCreate(&e);cudaEventRecord(s);for(int i=0;i<it;i++){chem<<<G,B>>>(a,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);update<<<G,B>>>(a,d,m,n,n,n,(size_t)n*n);std::swap(a,d);}cudaEventRecord(e);cudaEventSynchronize(e);float ms;cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,s,e);printf("%.3f ms %.3f MCUPS\n",ms,(double)N*it/ms/1000);}

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);

+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

codex
Implemented unconditional CUDA acceleration while preserving the `cahn_hilliard` executable name.

Key changes:

- Added optimized 3D CUDA stencil kernels for chemical potential and concentration updates.
- Kept simulation arrays resident on the GPU across iterations.
- Used coalesced warp-sized X dimensions, L1 cache preference, native GPU architecture, and CUDA event timing.
- Preserved clamped boundary conditions, initialization, validation, and result output semantics.
- Added CUDA error handling and safer argument/allocation checks.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_cuda_r5/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:1) and [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_cuda_r5/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:40).

Verification:

- Clean CUDA build succeeds.
- Default, irregular, degenerate, and zero-iteration cases pass validation.
- Results match the CPU reference within floating-point roundoff.
- RTX 3090 performance: approximately 6.6–8.1 billion cell updates/s, versus roughly 154 million for the original CPU implementation.
- Kernels use 26–28 registers with no spills.

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <utility>
+__device__ __forceinline__ int cl(int v,int n){return max(0,min(v,n-1));}
+__global__ void chem(const double* c,double* mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
+ double xx=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+c[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=c[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+c[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ mu[i]=4.5*((v+1)*(-2.0/9)+(v-1)*(-2.0/9)-2*v*(2.0/9))+3*v+v*v*v-.5*(xx+yy+zz);
+}
+__global__ void update(const double*c,double*n,const double*mu,int nx,int ny,int nz,size_t p){
+ int x=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x,y=blockIdx.y*blockDim.y+threadIdx.y,z=blockIdx.z*blockDim.z+threadIdx.z;if(x>=nx||y>=ny||z>=nz)return;
+ size_t i=(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+x;double v=mu[i];
+ double xx=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x+1,nx)]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)y*nx+cl(x-1,nx)]-2*v;
+ double yy=mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y+1,ny)*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)z*p+(size_t)cl(y-1,ny)*nx+x]-2*v;
+ double zz=mu[(size_t)cl(z+1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]+mu[(size_t)cl(z-1,nz)*p+(size_t)y*nx+x]-2*v;
+ n[i]=c[i]+.01*(xx+yy+zz);

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,
+                              double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                              size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double cv = c[index];
+    const double cxx =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+          clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double cyy =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    const double czz =
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        c[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+          static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv;
+    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+    constexpr double eAB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+    mu[index] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB -
+                       2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
 }

-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)
+void updateConcentration(const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                         double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                         const double* __restrict__ mu, int nx, int ny, int nz,
+                         size_t plane) {
+    const int x = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;
+    const int y = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int z = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * BLOCK_Z + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+                         static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;
+    const double center = mu[index];
+    const double mux =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x + 1, nx)] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx +
+           clampIndex(x - 1, nx)] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muy =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y + 1, ny)) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(y - 1, ny)) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double muz =
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z + 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] +
+        mu[static_cast<size_t>(clampIndex(z - 1, nz)) * plane +
+           static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] - 2.0 * center;
+    cnew[index] = cold[index] + 0.01 * (mux + muy + muz);
 }

+bool parseNumber(const char* text, size_t& value, bool allowZero) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno || end == text || *end != '\0' || (!allowZero && parsed == 0) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
+        return false;
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }
